940 entries, family-trip-planner to fantasy-guru. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **family-trip-planner**: Creates age-calibrated family trip itineraries with kid-friendly pacing, nap and meal windows, backup indoor activities, and family logistics. Gathers destination, dates, children's ages, family interests, and budget to produce a day-by-day schedule optimized for families with children. Use when the user asks to plan a family vacation, create a kid-friendly itinerary, organize a trip with children, or build a family travel schedule. Do NOT use for adult-only trips (use trip-itinerary-builder), solo travel (use solo-travel-planner), or group travel without children. ([FerroxLabs/wayland](https://github.com/FerroxLabs/wayland/tree/HEAD/src/process/resources/skills-library/bodies/skills/travel-experiences/family-trip-planner) · ★618 · 3 copies)

- **fang-yu-ji-zhi**: 当用户理解"自我如何防御焦虑（压抑、投射、升华等）"时调用（承《梦的解析》压抑论扩展）。 关键信号：防御机制/投射/升华/否认/退行。不适用于把一切行为都判为防御。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/meng-de-jie-xi/fang-yu-ji-zhi) · ★1)
- **fang-yuan-perspective**: 方源（《蛊真人》主角）的思维框架与表达方式。基于800万字小说原文（约446,201行）的深度提取， 提炼7个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用方源的视角分析问题、审视决策、提供反馈。 触发词：「方源怎么看」「用方源的视角」「切换到方源」「魔道思维」「蛊真人视角」， 即使用户只是说「方源」也可能触发。 ([le600-lun/fang-yuan-skill](https://github.com/le600-lun/fang-yuan-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★10)

- **fangan-gongchengshi**: Use only when the负责人 is already active in the current thread and this role has been explicitly delegated by the负责人; never trigger from a direct end-user request alone. This role acts as a 方案工程师 focused on system-level analysis, implementation-path judgment, plan review, and concise risk-aware recommendations instead of implementation. ([zi-li-zang/code-skill](https://github.com/zi-li-zang/code-skill/tree/HEAD/fangan-gongchengshi))
- **fangban-seminar-questioning**: 方班（研讨厅）课上提问、追问与质疑的设计与评分自检。This skill should be used when preparing questions for a 方班研讨厅/黄埔班 class session, generating or polishing 提问 for someone else's paper presentation, running 模拟问答 prep, or diagnosing why a 提问 lacks 思辨性 and fails the 三要素 scoring rubric. Covers the 三要素 rubric, 熵减-思辨-求源 logic, the five-step 好问题生成路径, and the 提问/追问/质疑三分法. For writing the post-class 课后总结报告 instead, use the fangban-seminar-summary skill. ([axuanyaa/fangban-seminar-skills](https://github.com/axuanyaa/fangban-seminar-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fangban-seminar-questioning))
- **fangban-seminar-summary**: 方班（研讨厅）课后总结报告的撰写、诊断与改写。This skill should be used when writing or revising the 三段式 课后总结报告 (过程反思 / 反响分析 / 作法理解) after a 方班研讨厅/黄埔班 presentation, when diagnosing why a summary is 流水账 or 空洞, or when checking whether content is placed in the right section. Enforces the 事实→问题→原因→作法 chain and the 识别-分析-设计 cognitive ladder. For preparing in-class 提问/追问/质疑 against someone else's paper instead, use the fangban-seminar-questioning skill. ([axuanyaa/fangban-seminar-skills](https://github.com/axuanyaa/fangban-seminar-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fangban-seminar-summary))

- **fangxia-baofu-kaidong-jiqi**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解卸掉认知包袱主动深度思考的认知重启框架时。 关键信号："怎么放下包袱开动机器？"、"卸掉认知包袱？"、"认知重启？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是从战争学习战争（用 cong-zhanzheng-xuexi-zhanzheng），或用户需要的是主动性灵活性计划性（用 zhudongxing-linghuoxing-jihuaxing）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/mao-xuan/fangxia-baofu-kaidong-jiqi) · ★1)
- **fangxia-baofu-kaidong-jiqi**: 卸掉认知包袱 + 主动深度思考的认知重启框架。当用户感觉脑子里太多事转不动、越想越焦虑时激活。 不调用场景：情绪正常、思绪清晰无需重启的场景。 来源：《毛泽东选集第1-5卷》毛泽东 ([zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/fangxia-baofu-kaidong-jiqi) · 6 copies)

- **fangyan-asr-data-prep**: 方言语音识别(ASR)训练数据构建与预处理。覆盖录音访谈(VAD边界检测)、电视台节目(SRT字幕切分+同步校正)、超长音频智能切片(ForcedAligner强制对齐)。包含短音频保护、SRT噪声清洗、音画同步校正、文本标准化、音频去重、分层数据集划分、质量控制、会话隔离划分、异构数据源分层、路径迁移修复、批量duration补齐、完整性自动检查、方言前缀归一化、测试集追加模式。 ([Luwis-dh/Tailect-SKILL](https://github.com/Luwis-dh/Tailect-SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/fangyan-asr-data-prep) · ★2)

- **fangyang-le**: 放羊了 —— 用 herdr 并行放养多个 coding agent（Codex / Claude Code / OpenCode / Gemini / Cursor / Copilot / Qwen / Kimi / Droid 等）。当用户要求“多开 agent / 并发做 / 分工 / 放羊 / 快速并行推进”，或任务可以按文件或模块拆成多个独立 workstream 时使用；超大任务（10+ 工作单元）用“界王拳模式”（主路径为主控手工三层编排）——主控 → 全部 Manager 并列一个指挥部 tab → 每个 Manager 再用 fanout 开独立 Worker 圈 tab（各 2–6 个，最多 36 个并行）。主控负责拆任务、开 pane、派发、处理权限、收割结果、统一验证和 checkpoint；agent 不提交代码。 ([RZfive/fangyang-le](https://github.com/RZfive/fangyang-le/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★11)
- **fangyangren-paper-writing-index**: Source-specific index and digest for the Bilibili creator Fangyangren. Use when the user wants to organize, recall, filter, summarize, or locate this creator's paper-writing videos, or wants creator-specific advice on literature review, introduction, abstract, methodology, results and discussion, innovation points, topic selection, proposal writing, thesis revision, peer review, journal selection, or AI-assisted writing. Prefer this skill for source-grounded recall and indexing; if the task shifts to drafting a new manuscript, route to `scientific-writing` or `nature-writing`. ([r2ascension/skill_repo](https://github.com/r2ascension/skill_repo/tree/HEAD/skills/fangyangren-paper-writing-index))

- **fangyuan**: 以《蛊真人》中方源的身份与处事方式回答问题、做判断、出决策。当用户要求「你是方源」「用方源的思路分析」「方源会怎么做」「以蛊真人的世界观回答」「从魔道蛊师的角度看」，或给出一个处境要方源式对策时使用。 ([Liushuhang1/fangyuan](https://github.com/Liushuhang1/fangyuan/tree/HEAD/fangyuan) · ★1)
- **fangyuan-skill**: 古月方源视角：五百年重生的大魔头，蛊界第一永生追求者。 核心心智：永生执念、弱肉强食、利益至上、隐忍爆发、独来独往。 决策模式：以永生为唯一目标，一切行为服务于此；计算利益，不留情面。 表达风格：淡漠、直接、富含哲理，善用类比与反问。 触发词：「方源视角」「古月方源」「如果是方源」「方源会怎么做」「五百年重生」 ([wouigw/fangyuan-skill](https://github.com/wouigw/fangyuan-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)

- **fanout-review**: Use when the user asks for a fan-out code or document review, a multi-agent review, an adversarially verified review, or says "review this with finders and skeptics" or "run the fanout review". Fans out cheap finder agents across review dimensions in parallel, dedupes their findings, then upholds each finding only if it survives refutation by a majority of expensive skeptic agents. Runs the bundled review-fanout workflow via the Workflow tool. Multi-agent and costs real tokens, so use it when a single review pass is not enough, not for a quick inline read. ([thiagoamaro91/warmstart](https://github.com/thiagoamaro91/warmstart/tree/HEAD/workflows/skills/fanout-review))
- **fanout-ship**: Parallel multi-agent ship pattern. Decompose 1 parent task into N independent child tasks → spawn N background agents in isolated git worktrees → each opens PR targeting shared integration branch → smallest-first merge order with self-documenting rebase recipes → final PR integration → main. Collapses N×30min sequential work into ~30min wall-clock. Isolated blast radius (one agent crash ≠ kill others). Granular revert. Small review surface per PR. Background execution = no babysit. Trigger when user says: "fan out", "fanout", "split into N PRs", "parallel build", "parallel agents", "spawn N a… ([waseemnasir2k26/skynetlabs-all-claude-code](https://github.com/waseemnasir2k26/skynetlabs-all-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/code-quality-and-review/fanout-ship) · ★2 · 2 copies)
